	float uvStart = samples <= 1.0 ? 0.0 : - 1.0;

function slqPackageIdFromFileName(fileName){
  var base=String(fileName||"").replace(/\.ifc$/i,"");
  var upper="-"+base.toUpperCase()+"-";
  var keys=Object.keys(SLQ_PACKAGE_REGISTER||{});
  for(var i=0;i<keys.length;i++){
    if(upper.indexOf("-"+keys[i].toUpperCase()+"-")!==-1) return keys[i];
  }
  var tokens=base.split("-");
  for(var j=0;j<tokens.length;j++){
    var tok=String(tokens[j]||"").trim().toUpperCase();
    if(!/^[A-Z0-9]{6}$/.test(tok)) continue;
    for(var k=0;k<keys.length;k++) if(keys[k].toUpperCase()===tok) return keys[k];
  }
  return "";
}
function mvcExport(includeLoinSummary){
  if(includeLoinSummary===undefined) includeLoinSummary=true;
  // Design package lookup table
  var PKG=SLQ_PACKAGE_REGISTER;
  // Auto-match Package ID from the IFC filename.
  // Rule: find a hyphen-delimited 6-character token (-XXXXXX-) that exists in the Package Register.
  // Matching is case-insensitive and uses the register itself, so it is not limited to a 2-digit + 4-letter pattern.
  var pkgCode='';var pkgName='';
  var _modelBase=(tt.fileName||'').replace(/\.ifc$/i,'');
  var _modelUpper=('-'+_modelBase.toUpperCase()+'-');
  var _pkgKeys=Object.keys(PKG);
  var _detectedPkg=slqPackageIdFromFileName(tt.fileName||'');
  if(_detectedPkg){
    for(var _dk=0;_dk<_pkgKeys.length;_dk++){
      if(_pkgKeys[_dk].toUpperCase()===_detectedPkg){pkgCode=_pkgKeys[_dk];pkgName=PKG[pkgCode]||'';break;}
    }
  }
  for(var _i=0;_i<_pkgKeys.length;_i++){
    var _candidate=_pkgKeys[_i].toUpperCase();
    if(_modelUpper.indexOf('-'+_candidate+'-')!==-1){
      pkgCode=_pkgKeys[_i];
      pkgName=PKG[pkgCode]||'';
      break;
    }
  }
  // Fallback: inspect every hyphen-delimited six-character token and compare it to register keys.
  if(!pkgCode){
    var _tokens=_modelBase.split('-');
    for(var _j=0;_j<_tokens.length;_j++){
      var _tok=String(_tokens[_j]||'').trim().toUpperCase();
      if(/^[A-Z0-9]{6}$/.test(_tok)){
        for(var _k0=0;_k0<_pkgKeys.length;_k0++){
          if(_pkgKeys[_k0].toUpperCase()===_tok){
            pkgCode=_pkgKeys[_k0];
            pkgName=PKG[pkgCode]||'';
            break;
          }
        }
        if(pkgCode)break;
      }
    }
  }

  // today in YYYY-MM-DD for the date input value
  var d=new Date();
  var todayISO=d.getFullYear()+"-"+String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,"0")+"-"+String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,"0");
  var modelNum=(tt.fileName||"").replace(/\.ifc$/i,"");
  var stage=(tt.results&&tt.results.stage)||"—";
  var modelSpan='<span style="color:#0b2940;font-weight:700">'+Ti(modelNum)+'</span>';

  var html="<!DOCTYPE html>\n<html lang=\"en\">\n<head>\n<meta charset=\"UTF-8\">\n<meta name=\"viewport\" content=\"width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0\">\n<title>Model Validation Certificate</title>\n<style>\n  * { box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; }\n  body { background: #c8c8c8; font-family: 'Calibri', Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 10pt; color: #222; }\n  .page-wrap { display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; gap: 20px; padding: 24px 0 40px; }\n\n  /* A4: 794 \u00d7 1123 px */\n  .page { width: 794px; min-height: 1123px; background:     '</div>';
  html=html.replace("<span class=\"ph\">CDS, PDS, FDS, PTU, IFC, ASC</span>",_gateDisplay);
  html=html.replace('</style>',"\n  /* MVC signature pad and package title preview */\n  .mvc-signature-wrap{width:100%;max-width:100%;min-height:100px}\n  #mvcSignature{display:block;width:100%;height:90px;background:#fff;border:1px dashed #7a9abf;border-radius:4px;cursor:crosshair;touch-action:none;}\n  .mvc-signature-actions{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:7px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:4px 0 0;color:#65768a;font-size:7pt}\n  .mvc-signature-actions button{border:1px solid #7a9abf;background:#f0f4fa;color:#0b2940;padding:3px 7px;border-radius:4px;cursor:pointer;font-size:7.5pt}\n  #mvcSignaturePrint{width:100%;height:90px;object-fit:contain;object-position:left center}\n  .pkg-title-preview{font-size:8pt;line-height:1.22;color:#536b85;white-space:normal;overflow-wrap:break-word;margin-top:4px}\n  @media print{.mvc-signature-actions{display:none!important}#mvcSignature{display:none!important}#mvcSignaturePrint[src]{display:block!important;border:0}#mvcSignaturePrint:not([src]){display:none!important}.mvc-signature-wrap{break-inside:avoid}}\n\n</style>");
  // MVC native calendar: default to today's local date, allow manual changes.
  // Browser calendar icon and click-to-open are supported; no input is read-only.
  var _mvcCalendarInput=function(id,label){
    return '<input type="date" id="'+id+'" class="mvc-calendar" '+
      'aria-label="'+label+'" title="'+label+' (defaults to today; choose a different date using the calendar)" '+
      'value="'+todayISO+'" '+
      'style="width:'+(id==='mvcReviewerDate'?'156px':'100%')+';max-width:100%;height:30px;min-height:30px;box-sizing:border-box;padding:4px 7px;'+
      'border:1px solid #b0b8c8;border-radius:4px;background:#fff;'+
      'font-size:9.5pt;font-family:Calibri,Arial,sans-serif;color:#0b2940;" '+
      'onclick="try{if(this.showPicker)this.showPicker()}catch(e){}">';
  };
  html=html.replace('<span class="ph">dd/mm/yyyy</span>',
    _mvcCalendarInput('mvcReviewDate','Review Date'));

  // A local handwritten signature pad is inserted in both the standalone MVC
  // and the final sign-off page of the combined report. It is optional.
  var _mvcSignatureMarkup=function(){
    return '<div class="mvc-signature-wrap">'+
      '<canvas id="mvcSignature" width="420" height="124" aria-label="Draw reviewer signature using mouse, pen or touch"></canvas>'+
      '<img id="mvcSignaturePrint" class="mvc-signature-print" alt="Reviewer signature" style="display:none" />'+
      '<div class="mvc-signature-actions no-print"><button type="button" id="mvcSignatureClear">Clear signature</button>'+
      '<span>Draw with mouse, stylus or touch</span></div>'+
      '</div>';
  };

  // The separate final Model Reviewer sign-off date uses the same calendar control.
  html=html.replace(
    '<tr><td><span class="ph">&lt;FULL NAME&gt;</span></td><td></td><td></td></tr>',
    '<tr><td><input type="text" id="mvcReviewerName" aria-label="Reviewer full name" placeholder="Full name" style="width:100%;border:1px solid #b0b8c8;border-radius:4px;height:30px;padding:4px 7px;font:9.5pt Calibri,Arial,sans-serif"></td><td>'+_mvcCalendarInput('mvcReviewerDate','Model Reviewer Date')+'</td><td>'+_mvcSignatureMarkup()+'</td></tr>'
  );
  // Inject model number into Native and Exchange rows
  html=html.replace("<span class=\"ph\">DSCRLP-XXXX-<span class=\"red\">XXX-XXXXXX</span>-M3D-000001</span>",modelSpan);
  html=html.replace("<span class=\"ph\">DSCRLP-XXXX-<span class=\"red\">XXX-XXXXXX</span>-M3D-000020</span>",modelSpan);
  // Replace all Yes/No cells with dropdowns
  html=html.split("<td class=\"yn ph\">Yes / No</td>").join("<td class=\"yn\"><select style=\"border:1px solid #b0b8c8;border-radius:4px;padding:2px 2px;width:86px;font-size:9pt;font-family:Calibri,Arial,sans-serif;color:#1d2129\"><option value=\"\">\u2014 select \u2014</option><option value=\"Yes\">Yes</option><option value=\"No\">No</option><option value=\"N/A\">N/A</option></select></td>");
  // Add print style to hide input border on print
  html=html.replace('</style>','@media print{.no-print,div[style*="position:fixed"]{display:none!important}input[type=date]{border:none;-webkit-appearance:none;appearance:none}}</style>');


  // --- Editable fields ---

  // Version field
  html=html.replace('<span class="ph">1A, 2A, 3A etc.</span>','<input type="text" placeholder="e.g. 1A" style="{INP}">'.replace('{INP}','border:1px solid #b0b8c8;border-radius:4px;width:100%;height:30px;box-sizing:border-box;font-size:9.5pt;font-family:Calibri,Arial,sans-serif;color:#0b2940;background:#fff;outline:none;padding:4px 7px;'));

  // Package Details table - Package Title dropdown linked to Package ID
  var _pkgTitleKeys=Object.keys(PKG).sort(function(a,b){
    return String(PKG[a]||'').localeCompare(String(PKG[b]||''));
  });
  var _pkgTitleOptions='<option value="">— Select Package Title —</option>';
  for(var _pti=0;_pti<_pkgTitleKeys.length;_pti++){
    var _ptk=_pkgTitleKeys[_pti];
    var _ptd=PKG[_ptk]||'';
    _pkgTitleOptions+='<option value="'+Ti(_ptk)+'"'+(pkgCode===_ptk?' selected':'')+'>'+Ti(_ptd)+'</option>';
  }
  var _pkgTitleSelect='<select id="_pkgtitle" '+
    'style="width:100%;box-sizing:border-box;background:#f0f4fa;border:1.5px solid #7a9abf;'+
    'border-radius:6px;padding:4px 9px;height:30px;font-size:9.5pt;font-family:Calibri,Arial,sans-serif;'+
    'color:#0b2940;font-weight:700;outline:none;cursor:pointer;" '+
    'onchange="(function(s){var p=document.getElementById(\'_pkgid\');if(p){p.value=s.value;}var o=s.options[s.selectedIndex];var preview=document.getElementById(\'_pkgtitlepreview\');if(preview)preview.textContent=o&&s.value?o.textContent:\'\';s.title=o&&s.value?o.textContent:\'\';})(this)">'+
    _pkgTitleOptions+'</select>';
  html=html.replace('<span class="ph">&lt;Selection list From Package Register&gt;</span>',_pkgTitleSelect+'<div class="pkg-title-preview" id="_pkgtitlepreview">'+Ti(pkgName||'')+'</div>');
  html=html.replace('<span class="ph">&lt;DISCIPLINE LEAD&gt;</span>','<input type="text" placeholder="Discipline Lead" style="{INP}">'.replace('{INP}','border:none;border-bottom:1px dashed #7a9abf;width:100%;font-size:10pt;font-family:Calibri,Arial,sans-serif;color:#0b2940;background:transparent;outline:none;padding:2px 4px;'));
  html=html.replace('<span class="ph">&lt;NAME of REVIEWER&gt;</span>','<input type="text" placeholder="Reviewer Name" style="{INP}">'.replace('{INP}','border:none;border-bottom:1px dashed #7a9abf;width:100%;font-size:10pt;font-family:Calibri,Arial,sans-serif;color:#0b2940;background:transparent;outline:none;padding:2px 4px;'));

  // Reviewer Full Name
  html=html.replace('<span class="ph">&lt;FULL NAME&gt;</span>','<input type="text" placeholder="Full Name" style="{INP}">'.replace('{INP}','border:none;border-bottom:1px dashed #7a9abf;width:100%;font-size:10pt;font-family:Calibri,Arial,sans-serif;color:#0b2940;background:transparent;outline:none;padding:2px 4px;'));

  // Free text comments - use textarea
  html=html.replace('<span class="ph">Free text</span>','<textarea placeholder="Additional notes..." style="{TA}"></textarea>'.replace('{TA}','border:1px dashed #7a9abf;border-radius:3px;width:100%;font-size:10pt;font-family:Calibri,Arial,sans-serif;color:#0b2940;background:transparent;outline:none;padding:4px 6px;resize:vertical;min-height:38px;box-sizing:border-box;'));

  // Inject JS at end of body to make ALL empty <td> cells editable
  var editScript='<script>'+
    'document.querySelectorAll("td").forEach(function(td){'+
      'if(td.innerHTML.trim()===""){'+
        'td.setAttribute("contenteditable","true");'+
        'td.style.outline="none";'+
        'td.style.cursor="text";'+
      '}'+
    '});'+
  '<\/script>';
  html=html.replace('</body>',editScript+'</body>');
  // Add a floating Print button (hidden when printing)
  var printBtn='<div style="position:fixed;bottom:28px;right:32px;z-index:999;no-print">'+
    '<button onclick="window.print()" style="background:#0b2940;color:#fff;border:none;border-radius:8px;padding:10px 28px;font-size:11pt;font-family:Calibri,Arial,sans-serif;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;box-shadow:0 4px 16px rgba(0,0,0,0.25);letter-spacing:.04em">'+
    '🖨 Print / Save PDF'+
    '</button>'+
  '</div>';
  html=html.replace('</body>',printBtn+'</body>');

  // Update print CSS to also hide editable borders
  // Comprehensive A4 print CSS - replaces any existing @media print block
  var printCSS=[
